6INCONCLUSIVE

What changed in this follow-up

The internal browser recovered. Admin-created isolated fixtures exercised 100 and 500 investors, exact 1000-dollar joins, unequal deposits, profitable 0% and 100% fee settlements, Real-Net pending/protection, 10-million-dollar sizing, margin call, automatic stop-out and UI/tab-close races. An isolated manager was temporarily changed to A-book: Black exchange filled one parent plus20 concurrent orders, and an LP-margin rejection created no orphan position. The fixture was restored to B-book after flat cleanup.

This is not a complete certification. Exact controlled price paths, CDP network faults, deterministic partial fills/requotes/provider outages, scheduled/module behavior and internal database/CPU evidence remain explicitly unverified. Functional scale completeness passes do not establish internal timing or transaction atomicity.

Correction: PAMM-FUNC-001 is withdrawn. The site's developer contract explicitly rounds exit payouts to 2 decimals; the two boundary observations match that contract. No new confirmed defect remains after contract review and exclusion of existing issues.
